# Conformance assertion matrix

Status: **harness self-tested only**. No provider execution is represented by this inventory.

Sole normative source: `rfcs/openrtc-core.md`. Base revision: `00d81f2`. Amended inventory: `v1-amended-780927ab`. RFC SHA-256: `780927ab73625607fa9530a5b6033a6cace3463a97f86ba8caf729d21a19a4f3`.

1353 obligation entries; 242 executable catalog definitions. Modal obligations are inventoried separately, including multiple obligations in one sentence. Appendix definitions, mandatory operation-table rows and §14.3 scenarios have separate entries.

Coverage describes the assertion mapping, never a passing execution. `partial` requires additional evidence for the rest of the clause; `missing` has no mapped executable assertion; `documentary` requires artifact, binding or independent evidence. A citation or historical alias alone does not establish coverage. SHOULD entries require an explicit decision and justification.

Final statuses are `pass`, `fail`, `incomplete`, and `not-applicable`. Missing fixtures, unrun assertions and unknown evidence are incomplete. Exclusions require the assertion, reason and evidence. Optional positive media tests apply only to the exact supported operation, kind, combination and owner scope; negative, security and cleanup obligations remain applicable. Claimed profiles require their mandatory operation positives.

## Executable catalog

Legacy CT identifiers are retained as aliases to revised assertions; historical passes cannot be reused for changed assertions. Legacy CORE/MED/etc citations are background crosswalk labels, not normative documents. Registry, descriptor, native-schema, Once/Off and inspection-ledger promises are bridge-specific and cannot make a universal RFC claim fail. A direct binding may use a harness-only adapter mapping.

| ID | Suite | Label | Current references / historical aliases | Observable oracle |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CT-REG-001 | core | common | ADP-002, ADP-100, ADP-101 | register() returns normally once; a second register of the same adapterId rejects with RTCError; initialize still works afterwards |
| CT-REG-002 | core | common | ADP-002, ADP-100, ADP-101, ADP-102 | register() or initialize() rejects with code incompatible-adapter; no client is created natively |
| CT-REG-003 | core | common | ADP-103, CFG-003, CFG-106, CFG-107 | adapter.schemas(l) and provider.schemas(l) return records naming adapter, lifetime, revision, JSON Schema and bindings |
| CT-REG-004 | core | common | ADP-101, ADP-106, SES-001 | native-effect counters for provisioning, admissions, captures and publications stay at their prior values |
| CT-REG-005 | core | common | ADP-001, ADP-004, ADP-110, CORE-103, CORE-105, CFG-113 | no administration factory/provisioner member on RTCProvider or RTCClient; secret-named fields in ClientConfig reject |
| CT-CFG-001 | core | common | CFG-001, CFG-002, CFG-102, CFG-105, ADP-116, ERR-103 | RTCError code unsupported-option naming the full optionPath, the lifetime and the adapter |
| CT-CFG-002 | core | common | CFG-001, CFG-002, ADP-004, ERR-103 | SessionProvisioner.create rejects unsupported-option with path and lifetime |
| CT-CFG-003 | core | common | CFG-001, CFG-003, CFG-105, CFG-106, ADP-103 | a payload for this adapter's namespace but the wrong lifetime, or with an unregistered schemaId, rejects with invalid-option or unsupported-option |
| CT-CFG-004 | core | common | CFG-001, CFG-100, CFG-102, CFG-113 | a field belonging to another lifetime (or unknown) in a holder rejects with optionPath naming it |
| CT-CFG-005 | core | common | CFG-001, CFG-100, SES-105 | after a rejected join/createClient, credential callbacks were not invoked and native counters are unchanged; no valid subset was applied |
| CT-CFG-006 | core | common | CFG-003, CFG-100, CFG-106 | validate({unknown field}) throws RTCError unsupported-option or invalid-option with a nonempty optionPath |
| CT-CFG-007 | core | conference | CFG-004, CFG-103 | a credential request issued after the application mutates its ClientConfig still carries the original subject |
| CT-CFG-008 | core | common | RFC §8.8:521–525, RFC §8.8:519–525, RFC §14.2:1102–1115, CFG-004, CFG-005, CFG-103, CFG-104, CFG-150 | configure() completes and inspection retains a ConfigurationReceipt whose fields never claim effective without accepted, or rejects with unsupported-option and a path |
| CT-CFG-009 | core | common | CFG-005, CFG-101 | for any schema binding with defaultValue status specified, a value is present; status unknown never carries a value |
| CT-SES-001 | core | conference | SES-001, SES-104, CORE-113 | after openSession: idle connection, unknown instance, no credential callback; native counters unchanged |
| CT-SES-002 | core | conference | RFC §7.2:117–131, RFC §8.4:479–483, SES-002, SES-105, STM-012, SES-117 | second join while pending rejects operation-conflict and the first completes; join after settlement rejects invalid-state; a fresh handle joins |
| CT-SES-003 | core | conference | RFC §7.2:117–169, RFC §8.5:487–500, RFC §8.6:506–510, SES-106, STM-002, STM-101, STM-102, STM-103 | Join result admission and the committed snapshot agree with the session; signaling connected does not imply admitted or active media |
| CT-SES-004 | core | conference | SES-108, SES-109, CORE-004, ADP-005 | The committed session snapshot lists the pre-existing remote endpoint, or declares the roster partial/unknown; no join event is awaited |
| CT-SES-005 | core | conference | SES-109, CORE-004, CORE-005, STM-104 | observedCount equals entries; native membership IDs carry adapter scope; participant IDs are unique |
| CT-SES-006 | core | conference | RFC §7.2:117–169, RFC §8.5:487–500, RFC §8.6:506–510, SES-106, STM-002, STM-102 | RFC §7.2:117–131: held join remains pending; admit yields admitted success on the same generation |
| CT-SES-007 | core | conference | SES-106, STM-102, STM-101 | join rejects authorization-denied, the generation becomes terminal, and the snapshot reports admission denied |
| CT-SES-008 | core | conference | SES-003, SES-100, SES-105 | per mode: join succeeds, or rejects unsupported-capability with no credential callback |
| CT-SES-009 | core | conference | SES-004, SES-101, SES-103 | SessionDefinition.allocation is resource or reservation; a session opened on it has no active instance; open() adds no provisioning call |
| CT-SES-010 | core | conference | RFC §9:544–570, SES-102, SES-103, SES-006, ERR-001 | join rejects with RTCError; broker called once on failure; original cause retained; no admission |
| CT-SES-011 | core | conference | SES-005, SES-119, CORE-153 | after the local session leaves, a remote endpoint is still connected and the instance is not reported ended |
| CT-EVT-001 | core | conference | CORE-006, CORE-108, CORE-109, CORE-111, STM-108 | recorded envelopes: one owner, the session generation, strictly increasing sequence, non-decreasing revision, shared transactions share a revision, valid origins |
| CT-EVT-002 | core | conference | CORE-109, STM-008, STM-108 | inside every listener, snapshot().revision >= envelope.revision, and equal revisions show the state the event reports |
| CT-EVT-003 | core | conference | CORE-006, CORE-109, STM-108 | a second listener still receives every connection event, and join still resolves |
| CT-EVT-004 | core | conference | CORE-110, CORE-112, STM-108 | a listener registered after join receives nothing until a new commit, then receives the new events |
| CT-EVT-005 | core | conference | CORE-110, CORE-007 | a once-listener whose handler calls leave() again, while a first leave() is already running, is invoked exactly once |
| CT-EVT-006 | core | conference | CORE-007, CORE-110 | no callback after unsubscribe/off returns; repeated unsubscribe and off of an absent listener do not throw; two registrations of one listener are both removed by off |
| CT-EVT-007 | core | conference | CORE-109, CORE-007, STM-108 | listener A calls leave() on 'connected'; listener B still sees 'connected' before 'leaving', in strictly increasing sequence |
| CT-EVT-008 | core | conference | STM-009, STM-108, SES-108, CORE-112 | first delivery is not older than the snapshot at registration; revisions strictly increase; the last delivery equals the final snapshot; none after unwatch |
| CT-CON-001 | core | conference | STM-101, STM-001, STM-117 | every connectionChanged edge is listed in the table; each previous equals the prior current; ended/failed are final |
| CT-CON-002 | core | conference | STM-002, SES-107, MED-001 | with captureDefaults set and no initialMedia, no capture or publication exists after join |
| CT-CON-003 | core | conference | STM-001, SES-002, SES-117, STM-117 | after leave: terminal state, unchanged generation, no later connection events, join rejects; a fresh handle gets a new generation |
| CT-CON-004 | core | conference | STM-003, STM-101, SES-116, SES-118 | connection reports recovering with planes and a known cause/continuity enum, then connected on the same generation; ended/failed only if recovery truly failed |
| CT-ID-001 | core | conference | RFC §4:23; §8.3:477, RFC §4:23, RFC §6:33–35, RFC §8.2:461–463, CORE-004, CORE-005, CORE-107, SES-110 | a remote's display name never appears as its application identity; participant ids are scoped; the local application identity is not rewritten |
| CT-ID-002 | core | conference | RFC §8.2:461–463; §14.3:1134, RFC §8.2:461–463, RFC §14.3:1134, SES-007, SES-110, CORE-005 | two clients with one identity: reject-new rejects the second; replace-existing ends the first; allow-multiple keeps both; unknown is reported unknown |
| CT-CRD-001 | core | conference | SES-111, CFG-009, SES-009 | first callback: get, phase admission, reason initial, this adapter, the client's subject, session-admission scope with this adapter's address |
| CT-CRD-002 | core | conference | RFC §9:544–546, RFC §7.2:117–169, RFC §8.5:487–500, RFC §8.6:506–510, SES-008, SES-112, ERR-001, ERR-109 | join rejects credential-failed; the callback's own error is retained; no admission; no automatic retry |
| CT-CRD-003 | core | conference | ERR-109, ERR-102, SES-111 | join rejects with code authentication-failed and category authentication, never authorization-denied |
| CT-CRD-004 | core | conference | CFG-009, CFG-114, ERR-107 | a deep scan of snapshots, recorded envelopes, capability snapshots and JSON of failures finds none of the credential's UTF-8 or base64 text |
| CT-CRD-005 | core | conference | SES-009, SES-010, SES-113, SES-115, STM-111, CFG-115 | replacement phase request with unchanged subject/scope; credentialChanged carries a result kind permitted by the advertised mode; the session stays connected |
| CT-CRD-006 | core | conference | SES-008, SES-112, STM-006, STM-111, ERR-003, ERR-101 | credentialChanged and an observation-kind error arrive; the connection stays connected; validity is not expired/revoked unless the expiry is known |
| CT-CRD-007 | core | conference | SES-114, STM-010, STM-011 | cancel aborts the callback's signal; after the late credential is released the session is not connected and no admission occurred |
| CT-CAN-001 | core | conference | SES-121, STM-010 | result rejects cancelled; no credential callback; native admissions and captures unchanged |

## Evidence limits

Every unmapped applicable assertion is incomplete, including unimplemented profile-positive, protection, stream, authorization and published-artifact checks. Local operation settlement is separate from remote cleanup. A native request acknowledgment cannot prove release; unknown remote state remains unknown. A fake cannot establish a real service boundary or artifact audit. Public representation equivalence requires a documented mapping to actual implementation operations and facts.
